fc270bad2b4cb3eb86d5ed27aa75809e7c555543: $$Q_t = \begin{bmatrix}
q(x_t^1|x_{t-1}^1) & q(x_t^2|x_{t-1}^1) & \cdots & q(x_t^K|x_{t-1}^1) \\
\vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots \\
q(x_t^1|x_{t-1}^i) & q(x_t^2|x_{t-1}^i) & \cdots & q(x_t^K|x_{t-1}^i) \\
\vdots & \vdots & \ddots & \vdots \\
q(x_t^1|x_{t-1}^K) & q(x_t^2|x_{t-1}^K) & \cdots & q(x_t^K|x_{t-1}^K)
\end{bmatrix}$$

2b2bb8fb8fdb01e5ae357b8147a57c7337f009cc: $$q(x^i_t|x^j_{t-1})$$

fc9317fda5aa3803b91ef98eefb7549470fa4638: $$x_t, x_{t-1} \in \{1, \ldots, K\}$$

0612b00d9fe4a51d7a4778e41c14718bfe4ffbc2: $$q(x_t|x_0)=\text{Cat}(x_t;p=x_0Q_{0:t})$$

880963a71c685f59ee03fd18bfc8f0fa7bb60453: $$q(x_t^i | x_0^i) = \begin{cases}
1 - t, & x_t^i = x_0^i \\
t, & x_t^i = \mathbf{M}
\end{cases}$$

9e3aea7397c399caf59e4d969ca888372515bd88: $$q(x_s|x_t) = \prod_{i=1}^{L} q(x_s^i|x_t^i)$$

bb0b7ea819d7a1dcec99ea2a9817523814004bd5: $$q(x_s^i | x_t^i) = \begin{cases}
1, & x_t^i \neq \mathbf{M}, \ x_s^i = x_t^i \\
\frac{s}{t}, & x_t^i = \mathbf{M}, \ x_s^i = \mathbf{M} \\
\frac{t-s}{t} \, p_\theta(x_0^i | x_t), & x_t^i = \mathbf{M}, \ x_s^i \neq \mathbf{M} \\
0, & \text{else}
\end{cases}$$

67b3aa924057bd226c63d5c713e62db725517346: $$\mathcal{L}_{\text{DLM}} = -\mathbb{E}_{x_0, x_t, t} \left[ \frac{1}{t} \sum_{i=1}^{L} \mathbb{1}\{x_t^i = M\} \log p_\theta(x_0^i | x_t) \right]$$
